## st-0133 — The Dog and His Reflection
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
One man has a pet. The pet lives near a small town. The pet is very big. The pet is very empty inside. He wants some food. He looks for food. He looks for a long time. He wants something to eat. He finds a big meal. He takes the big meal. He walks back home now. He has food in his hand.

He walks on a way. He walks on a small place. A clear water area is under him. Water moves down. The pet looks down. He sees water. He sees himself in water. He does not understand it. He thinks it is another pet. He sees another thing. He wants that thing too. He is very bad.

He wants his food good. He opens his part. He tries to take more. His meal falls down. The meal goes into water. The meal is lost. He sees nothing. The pet has nothing now. He feels bad. He gets a big fact. He understands a lot.

### A2
Once upon a time, a dog lived near a town. The dog felt very low on food. He went out to find some good food. After a long time, he found a big piece. He took the piece in his face. Then he started walking back home to his place. He was going to go back to his house. He had a big goal in his mind. To reach home, he walked over a road. The road crossed a deep, clear water area. Water moved down under the road. The dog looked down into the water. What he saw surprised him very much. He saw his own picture in the water. He did not know about pictures in water. He thought it was another dog. He believed the other dog had a bigger piece. Want of more filled his head. He wanted that other piece too.

He should have kept his own piece safe. Instead, the dog decided to attack the picture. He opened his face wide. This was a bad issue. His own piece fell down into the water. The piece went deep into the water. It was lost now. The picture went away. The dog finally understood his bad choice. He ended up with nothing in the end. He tried to take more than he needed. The light went down in the high air. The day was over for the animal.

He walked home with empty hands. He did not have any food left. His family was waiting for him at home. They were going to be sad about the food. The dog learned a thing today. He would never do that again. He must be happy with what he has. Want of more only brings trouble to everyone. Now he knows the real fact about life.

### B1
Once upon a time, a dog lived near a small town. The dog wanted food because he had not eaten for a long time. He went out to find something good to eat today. After searching for hours, he found a big bone in the dirt. He took the bone carefully in his mouth. Then, he started walking home down the long road. To reach his house, the dog had to walk over a bridge. The bridge crossed a clear river where water flowed slowly. As he walked, the dog looked down into the water below. What he saw down there surprised him greatly. In the water, the dog saw his own face looking back at him. He did not understand how that worked in this world. He thought it was another dog holding a much bigger bone. He wanted that other bone too, because he always wanted more. If he had been careful, he would have kept his own food safe. Instead, the dog decided to move quickly across the bridge. He opened his mouth wide to attack, which was a terrible mistake. The moment he opened his mouth, his own bone fell into the river. The bone went deep into the water and sank down. It was lost and he never saw it again. When the water became clear, the dog realized what he had done.

He ended up with nothing because he tried to take more than he needed. This simple story teaches us that extra things can cost us what we already have. He has learned a hard lesson about life today. If he finds food again, he will not drop it. The dog will keep his meal safe in his mouth. Many people tell this story to children in the town every single day. Every person understands the important meaning behind the story. We must always appreciate what we hold in our hands today. Wanting too much brings only loss and pain in the end. So let us stay happy with our own share of life. The dog has walked home with empty hands and a sad heart. He will remember this day for a long time. Life is short and we should be happy with little things. If everyone shares, nobody will have to suffer. The river still flows under the old bridge. Children play near the water and talk about the funny dog. They know that dogs cannot think like people do. Yet the story remains important for all of us. We have heard it many times from our parents. It helps us understand how to live a good life.

### B2
Once upon a time, a dog lived happily near a small town. The dog felt very low on food. Because he wanted something to eat, he went out to find food. After searching for a long time, he found a big, wonderful bone. He took the bone in his mouth and started walking back home. To reach his house, the dog had to walk over a bridge. The bridge crossed a deep, clear river where the water flowed very slowly. As he walked across the bridge, the dog looked down into the water. What he saw down there surprised him greatly. In the water, the dog saw his own reflection looking back up at him. Because he did not understand how a reflection worked, he thought it was another dog. Even worse, he believed that the other dog was holding a much bigger bone. Strong desire quickly filled his mind, and he wanted that other bone too.

If he had been smart, he would have kept his own food safe in his mouth. Instead, the silly dog decided to snap at the reflection in the water. He opened his mouth wide to attack the other animal, which was a terrible mistake. The moment he opened his mouth, his own bone fell out and dropped straight into the river. The bone sank deep into the water and was lost forever. When the reflection disappeared, the dog finally realized what he had done. He ended up with nothing because he had tried to take more than he needed. This simple story teaches us that reaching for extra things can cost us what we already have. Many people live their lives without understanding this simple lesson. If we always want more than we need, we might lose our own property. This situation happens every single day in the real world. People look at what others have and want those things too. They try to take everything, but they end up with nothing in the end. We should be happy with what we already possess. Taking care of our own food and home is much better than fighting for something else. The river flows day and night under the old bridge. Fish swim in the clear water while the world moves forward. Nobody can change the past once a mistake is made.

The dog learned his lesson, but it was already too late for him. He walked away slowly with an empty mouth and a heavy heart. His action brought him only trouble and dark thoughts. Every reader should think about this story and learn from it. If you are smart, you will keep what is yours. Do not try to take things that belong to someone else. Otherwise, you might lose everything that matters to you in life. We often see people who want everything they can reach. They lose their focus and forget about important values. Money and power cannot replace a quiet, calm mind. Smart people know that taking care of friends is better than fighting. The world is full of choices that shape our daily experience. When we make a decision, we must accept the result. Every single person can build a better future through careful thought. Life offers many chances to learn from past mistakes. We should always try to be kind and helpful. Sharing what we have brings joy to our community. There is no reason to fight over material things. Peace and quiet are worth more than any large bone. Let us remember this important lesson as we go through life. Good friends and family matter more than extra wealth. If we keep our minds open, we will grow wiser each day. The sun rises every morning and brings new light to the world.

Birds sing in the trees while people start their daily work. We should appreciate every single moment we are given. Time passes quickly, and we cannot bring back yesterday. Therefore, we must live each day with a clean heart. Help your neighbors when they need support. Do not let want take control of your actions. True happiness comes from within, not from what we own. May this story stay in your mind for a long time. Thank you for reading about the dog and the bridge. May you always make wise choices in your own life. Good friends and family matter more than extra wealth. If we keep our minds open, we will grow wiser each day. The sun rises every morning and brings new light to the world. Birds sing in the trees while people start their daily work. We should appreciate every single moment we are given. Time passes quickly, and we cannot bring back yesterday. Therefore, we must live each day with a clean heart. Help your neighbors when they need support. Do not let want take control of your actions. True happiness comes from within, not from what we own. May this story stay in your mind for a long time. Thank you for reading about the dog and the bridge. May you always make wise choices in your own life.

### C1
Once upon a time, a dog lived happily near a small town, finding comfort in the quiet rhythms of the countryside. The dog felt very low on food as winter approached and resources grew scarce across the fields. Because he wanted something to eat, he ventured out into the chilly air to find sustenance. After searching for a long time through overgrown paths and empty ditches, he finally found a big, wonderful bone. It was a treasure of marrow and rich promise, glistening in the pale afternoon light. He took the bone firmly in his mouth and started walking back home, his spirit lifted by unexpected fortune.

To reach his house on the eastern hill, the dog had to walk over an old stone bridge. The bridge crossed a deep, clear river where the water flowed very slowly beneath the arches. As he walked across the bridge, the dog looked down into the water out of idle curiosity. What he saw down there surprised him greatly, arresting his attention entirely. In the crystal mirror of the water, the dog saw his own reflection looking back up at him. Because he did not understand how a reflection worked, his simple mind assumed it was another dog. Even worse, through some strange distortion of perception, he believed that the other dog was holding a much bigger bone.

Strong desire quickly filled his mind, casting a sudden shadow over his previous contentment. He wanted that other bone too, convinced that he deserved the larger prize. If he had been smart and measured in his judgment, he would have kept his own food safe in his mouth. Instead, the silly dog decided to snap at the reflection in the water with sudden fury. He opened his mouth wide to attack the perceived rival, which was a terrible and irreversible mistake. The moment he opened his mouth to bark, his own bone fell out and dropped straight into the river.

The heavy bone sank deep into the water and was lost forever beneath the murky weeds. When the ripple cleared and the reflection disappeared, the dog finally realized the full extent of what he had done. He ended up with nothing because he had tried to take more than he needed in a fit of greed. This simple story teaches us that reaching for extra things can easily cost us what we already have. Many people live their lives without ever understanding this simple lesson, blinded by perpetual want.

If we always want more than we need, we might lose our own property in the pursuit of illusions. This frustrating situation happens every single day in the real world among ambitious souls. People look at what others have and want those unnecessary things too, driven by envy. They try to take everything within their reach, but they end up with nothing in the end. We should be truly happy with what we already possess rather than chasing shadows. Taking care of our own food and home is much better than fighting for something else.

The river flows day and night under the old bridge, indifferent to human folly and animal mistakes. Fish swim in the clear water while the world moves forward through its endless cycles. Nobody can change the past once a foolish mistake is made and the consequences unfold. The dog learned his bitter lesson, but it was already far too late for him to recover his prize. He walked away slowly with an empty mouth and a heavy heart, dragging his paws in the dust. His careless action brought him only bitter trouble and dark thoughts that lingered long after sunset.

Every reader should think about this classic story and learn from its enduring wisdom. If you are smart, you will keep what is yours and cherish your secure position. Do not try to take things that belong to someone else, for jealousy breeds ruin. Otherwise, you might lose everything that matters to you in life before you even notice. We often see people who want everything they can reach, no matter the cost. They lose their focus entirely and forget about important values that sustain human dignity.

Money and power cannot replace a quiet, calm mind and a contented spirit. Smart people know that taking care of friends is much better than fighting over material gains. The world is full of choices that shape our daily experience and define our character. When we make a decision, we must accept the result with grace and maturity. Every single person can build a better future through careful thought and disciplined action. Life offers many chances to learn from past mistakes if we remain humble enough to listen.

We should always try to be kind and helpful to those who share our journey. Sharing what we have brings genuine joy to our community and strengthens the bonds between neighbors. There is no reason to fight over material things that ultimately hold no lasting value. Peace and quiet are worth more than any large bone or temporary luxury. Let us remember this important lesson as we go through life and face its temptations. Good friends and family matter more than extra wealth or hollow recognition.

If we keep our minds open, we will grow wiser each day and see through deceitful desires. The sun rises every morning and brings new light to the world, offering a clean slate. Birds sing in the trees while people start their daily work with renewed dedication. We should appreciate every single moment we are given before time slips through our fingers. Time passes quickly, and we cannot bring back yesterday no matter how much we weep. Therefore, we must live each day with a clean heart and a grateful attitude.

Help your neighbors when they need support, and lift them up during difficult seasons. Do not let want take control of your actions or turn your heart toward bitterness. True happiness comes from within, rooted in peace, rather than from what we own. May this story stay in your mind for a long time as a gentle warning. Thank you for reading about the dog and the bridge and his foolish adventure. May you always make wise choices in your own life and protect what truly matters.

### C1
For weeks, a desperate creature wandered the desolate fringes of the pasture, consumed by a relentless hunger that demanded sustenance with every passing dawn. Day after day, his hollow stomach ached as he surveyed the lush meadow from afar, tormented by the impossibility of seizing even a single meal. The shepherd, vigilant and unyielding, stood guard over his precious flock with unwavering devotion. Not a single moment passed without the watchful guardian scanning the horizon, his keen eyes missing nothing that moved upon the grass. Every member of the flock remained entirely safe beneath that protective gaze, shielded from any threat that dared to approach.

Life had grown profoundly difficult for the starving predator, who found himself utterly excluded from the abundance he so desperately craved. He knew all too well that any conventional approach would result in swift and certain failure against such a watchful opponent. Hours bled into one another as he sat motionless in the tall grass, watching the sun traverse the vast, indifferent sky. Though his hope dwindled to a fragile ember, the intensity of his desire for food never once faltered. Every minute spent in the shadows brought new, calculated thoughts about how he might bypass the shepherd and breach the safety of the flock.

The sheep drifted slowly across the emerald expanse, grazing in tranquil ignorance while the shepherd paced methodically behind them. Nothing unusual disturbed the long, languid hours of the afternoon, save for the low hum of insects and the sigh of the wind. From his hidden vantage point at a distance, the creature tracked their every movement with predatory precision. As the afternoon waned, the crisp autumn air grew steadily colder, signaling the approach of evening across the open field. The shepherd began to gather his charges, preparing to guide them back to the security of the fold before total darkness fell.

The creature waited patiently as the last bruised remnants of daylight faded entirely from the darkening sky. Hours passed in profound silence while the lonely predator remained hidden in the gloom, contemplating his gnawing hunger. He nursed the faint, persistent hope that tomorrow might somehow present a more favorable opportunity for success. Yet the biting chill of the night air reminded him that survival demanded immediate, drastic action rather than passive waiting. Restless and determined, he resolved to remain near the pasture and ponder his dilemma beneath the indifferent stars.

One fine morning, fate intervened as the smart creature stumbled upon a discarded, weather-worn fleece lying neglected on the damp ground. Instantly, a cunning strategy began to form in his sharp mind, promising a clever solution to his persistent problem. He gathered the heavy, woolly garment and draped it carefully over his own lean frame, adjusting it until it fit like a second skin. Stepping cautiously forward, he integrated himself seamlessly among the grazing animals, moving with an eerie, unearned confidence. No one suspected that a dangerous hunter was now standing right beside them, concealed beneath the stolen texture of innocence.

This ingenious trick worked remarkably well for a time, allowing the imposter to hide securely in plain sight. The other sheep remained entirely calm, completely deceived by the familiar texture and scent of the wool. Even the watchful shepherd glanced across the meadow without noticing the slightest difference among his charges. An intoxicating wave of triumph washed over the creature, convincing him that his audacious plan was destined for total victory. He walked slowly and quietly through the grass, ensuring that no sudden movement would betray his true identity to the world.

All around him, the sheep grazed peacefully, their eyes devoid of any hint of fear or suspicion. The shepherd stood nearby with a serene expression, believing that all was well within the boundaries of his domain. Everything proceeded precisely according to the ambitious design the creature had formulated earlier that morning. Hours drifted by pleasantly as the disguised predator lingered comfortably within the heart of the unsuspecting group. He felt thoroughly convinced that his daring gamble would soon be rewarded with an effortless and satisfying meal.

Slowly, the sun sank below the distant hills, casting long, dramatic shadows that stretched across the darkening grass. The fading light dissolved into dusk, and the cool evening breeze whispered softly through the branches of the surrounding trees. The shepherd raised his voice and signaled his animals, beginning the familiar routine of gathering them for the journey home. Remaining preternaturally calm, the clever creature waited for the precise moment to execute the final phase of his plan. He felt absolute confidence that nobody would ever pierce the disguise or discover who he truly was.

Yet, as irony would dictate, his own elaborate deception ultimately became the direct instrument of his downfall. When evening arrived, the shepherd needed to select an animal from the flock to provide for his evening needs. Reaching into the gathering gloom, the man mistakenly seized the strange, wool-covered figure, entirely believing it to be a sheep. This astonishing outcome vividly demonstrated how easily a false appearance can conceal a treacherous and dangerous heart. A dishonest scheme, no matter how carefully constructed, will inevitably bring unexpected ruin to its creator.

The shepherd pulled the disguised creature firmly away from the rest of the flock and into the fading light. It was only then, upon closer inspection, that the shocking truth became glaringly apparent to the astonished man. He stared in disbelief at the sharp teeth and predatory eyes revealed beneath the heavy, borrowed fleece. A surge of intense surprise and righteous anger swept through the shepherd as he comprehended the gravity of the situation. The trapped creature realized with sudden horror that his once-foolproof plan had disintegrated into utter failure.

Realizing that escape was entirely impossible, the defeated predator stood frozen as the shepherd took decisive action to neutralize the threat. This remarkable event serves as an enduring reminder of how crucial it is to look far beyond deceptive surfaces. A clever trick may succeed temporarily, but the unyielding truth eventually rises to the surface to expose every falsehood. The foolish creature learned a harsh, unforgettable lesson on that cold evening as peace gradually returned to the quiet countryside. The shepherd safely led his genuine flock home, ensuring that the land remained secure and undisturbed for many years to come.

## st-0135 — The Milkmaid and Her Pail
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A young girl gets up early today. She carries water to the market now. She puts it on her head right here. She feels very good in her mind. She walks along the country way. She thinks about her big plans.

She will sell water for money. She plans to buy many small things. Those things will become little life. She will grow them on family land. She will sell them at market later. She will buy a new house too.

She thinks about being at a party. People will look at her very much. She sees herself moving right then. Other people try to get her view. Then she moves her head up high. She says no to them with a look.

She gives a smile to those people. She moves her head back down now. The water goes off down the way. It falls hard on the dark land. All white food spills out of it. The food is gone from her hand.

Her big plans go away from her. She stands by the way right now. She looks down with much hurt. She learns a very hard thing today. She walks home to family again. She has no money in her hand.

She thinks about the hard problem. She will be more good next time. She learns from her big mistake. She walks home with her family. She knows life is long for her.

### A2
One young girl got up early one morning. She carried something on her head. It was a large thing with food inside. She walked down the road to market. She felt very good about her future. Her mind began to dream about money. She thought about money from the food. She planned to buy little things soon. Then she wanted to buy some small things. Those things would grow up on a land. She would sell the big things at market. That money would buy a fine nice dress. She imagined wearing the dress to a party. She would look good while people watched. The girl pictured herself dancing with boys. She would move her head and say no. She forgot about the thing on her head.

Suddenly, she moved her head back. The thing moved off her head. It fell hard down to the ground. All the food went out. Nothing remained in the container. Her great plans went away in a second. She stood alone by the road in deep sadness. She learned a hard task that day. Nobody should trust things before they happen. She was very sorry about her wrong act. She walked back home to her family. Her mother told her to be careful. The girl understood the task well. She would never make that wrong act again. She planned to work hard every day. Her future would be good and fine. She would save money for real needs. She would always keep her mind clear. Life went on for the young girl. She is going to be fine now.

### B1
A young girl woke up early one bright morning. She carried a heavy metal container of milk toward the market. She placed the container carefully upon her head. She felt very proud of the fresh white food inside. She walked along the quiet country road. Her mind began to move into nice thoughts about her future. She thought about how much money she would receive. She planned to sell all the milk to people in town. With that money, she would buy a large number of eggs. Those eggs would soon grow into many small birds. She would feed and grow them on her family farm. She has always dreamed about a wonderful life. If she sells the milk, she will buy the eggs. She has worked hard for this great goal. Every single day brings new hope to her heart. She walks slowly while thinking about her future.

Once the birds were big enough, she would sell them. She would get a much higher price at the busy market. That extra money would allow her to buy a beautiful new dress. The dress would be made of fine material. She imagined wearing that fine material to the next village party. Everyone would look at her in wonder. The young girl pictured herself dancing happily. Young boys would try to catch her attention. She imagined that she would push her head proudly in the air. She would say no to them with a smile. She forgot about the heavy container resting on her head. She has imagined this scene many times before. If she meets the boys, she will smile at them. She has kept her balance for a long time. The market is full of people waiting today. She feels very happy about her good plan.

Suddenly, she actually pushed her head back. The heavy container slipped off right then. It crashed hard onto the ground below. All the white milk went out across the earth. Nothing remained inside the empty metal container. If she had not moved her head, she would have kept the milk. With the milk gone, all her great plans went away. She stood alone by the road. She stared down at the ground with deep trouble and worry. She learned a hard lesson that day. Nobody should ever count on things before they actually happen. She has lost everything in one single moment. If she had stayed still, she would have succeeded. She has learned a very important life lesson. Now she stands by the road with deep sadness. 她的 big dreams have gone into thin air.

### B2
A young girl woke up early one bright morning. She carried a heavy metal container of fresh white liquid toward the market. She placed the metal container carefully upon her head. She felt very proud of the fresh white liquid inside. As she walked along the quiet country road, her mind began to wander. She had pleasant thoughts about her future. She thought about how much money she would receive. She would sell all the liquid to people in town. With that money, she planned to buy a large number of eggs. A local farmer would sell those eggs to her. Those eggs would soon grow into many small chickens. She would feed and grow them on her family farm. Once the chickens were big enough, she would sell them. She would get a much higher price at the busy market. That extra money would allow her to buy a beautiful new dress. The dress would be made of fine material. She imagined wearing that wonderful clothing to the next village party. Everyone would look at her in wonder. The young girl pictured herself dancing happily. The young boys would try to catch her attention. She imagined that she would move her head proudly in the air. She would say no to them with a smile.

Lost entirely inside this wonderful dream, she forgot about the heavy container. It was resting balanced on her head. Suddenly, she actually moved her head back. She had done that exact thing in her mind. The heavy metal container slipped off immediately. It crashed hard onto the dirty ground below. All the white liquid fell out across the earth. Nothing was left behind in the empty metal container. With the liquid gone forever, all her brilliant plans disappeared. They vanished into thin air within a single second. She stood alone by the road. She stared down at the wet ground with deep regret. She learned a hard lesson that day. She realized that nobody should ever count on things before they actually happen. She walked home slowly. She was thinking about her mistakes. The sun was shining brightly in the sky. However, she felt very cold inside. Her mother would surely ask about the empty metal container. What would she tell her family? She had lost everything in a single moment. That loss happened because of her silly imagination.

Life is full of strange turns. People must remain careful. If she had kept her mind on the road, the liquid would have reached the market. It would have arrived safely. Instead, she lost both the liquid and her future plans at the exact same time. Many other young people make similar mistakes every single day. They build huge dreams upon foundations that do not yet exist. Experience is often the best teacher that anyone can ever find. She promised herself that she would never act so silly again. From that morning forward, she decided to focus only on reality. She would avoid empty dreams. The road ahead was long. She knew that hard work would bring success eventually.

She arrived home with an empty container and a heavy heart. Her mother looked at her with concern. She asked what had happened on the way. The girl explained everything honestly. She held back her tears with great effort. Her mother smiled gently. She told her not to worry too much about the past. Mistakes happen to everyone. Knowledge is gained through such difficult trials. With strong power, the young girl started to plan for another day. She knew that tomorrow would bring new opportunities. She needed to stay focused and work hard. The country road remained quiet. The afternoon sun began to fall slowly behind the hills.

She sat down by the window. She watched the birds fly across the open sky. Her mind was clear now. It was free from all the previous silly thoughts. She understood that real success requires care and attention. Constant work is also necessary. Every step she took from that moment forward was guided by reason. Common sense also guided her path. She would always remember the lesson learned on that bright morning. Life would bring many other challenges. She was ready to face them with an open mind. Her family would support her through every single difficulty. She looked out at the garden. The green plants were growing tall in the warm sun. There was still much work to do before the end of the year. She took a deep breath and smiled at the future. Nothing could stop her from achieving her goals. She remained dedicated and focused on her path. The dark clouds of the past had cleared away completely. They left only bright hope for tomorrow.

### C1
The morning broke with a crisp, amber clarity over the dew-drenched meadows, promising a day of relentless heat and industrious motion. Patty stepped across the threshold of her modest cottage before the sun had fully cleared the eastern ridge, her mind already tethered to the bustling marketplace miles away. Balanced securely upon the crown of her head rested a heavy galvanized pail, filled to its brim with rich, foaming milk freshly drawn from the family's prized cow. She felt a quiet surge of pride as she adjusted her posture, acutely aware of the precious cargo and the potential it represented for her household. The country road stretched before her like a pale ribbon, flanked by rustling hedgerows and silent fields waiting for the touch of noon.

As her boots crunched rhythmically against the gravel, the solitude of the lane invited her thoughts to drift inward toward grander horizons. She began to calculate the exact sum the milk would fetch once she handed it over to the eager merchants in the town square. With those coins safely tucked into her apron pocket, she would immediately seek out the local poultry farmer whose yard was known for the healthiest breeds. A generous portion of her earnings would exchange hands, securing a dozen speckled eggs that she would hatch under the watchful eye of an old brooding hen. Before long, those delicate shells would crack to reveal a bustling flock of yellow chicks peeping eagerly in the straw.

She pictured herself tending to the growing brood behind the barn, mixing grain and water through the long afternoons of early summer. Once fully matured, those very birds would command a formidable price at the autumn fair when townspeople gathered to stock their winter larders. The profit from such a sale would dwarf her initial earnings, opening the door to desires she had previously only dared to whisper in the dark. With that substantial wealth, she resolved to commission the village tailor to craft a magnificent dress from fine, imported silk. The material would shimmer softly in the candlelight of the upcoming autumn festival, catching the gaze of every villager who crossed her path.

Lost entirely within the intoxicating warmth of this daydream, she forgot the physical reality of the metallic weight resting upon her brow. She imagined the swelling cadence of the village fiddles, the whirl of dancers moving in graceful patterns across the wooden floorboards. Young men of good standing would undoubtedly approach, whispering flattering words and seeking to claim her hand for the next lively reel. In the theater of her mind, she felt a sudden surge of playful arrogance, lifting her chin with a haughty toss of her dark curls. To complete the vivid performance of her imagination, she jerked her head backward in a sharp gesture of elegant refusal, murmuring a decisive word of dismissal.

The phantom motion shattered the delicate equilibrium that kept the heavy pail balanced safely atop her head. With a sudden, sickening metallic scrape, the container pitched sideways and slipped from its precarious perch into the open air. It struck the unforgiving stones of the road with a resounding crash that echoed sharply against the quiet hillsides. Within a single fraction of a second, the rich white milk spilled in a wide, foaming torrent across the dusty earth. Nothing remained within the dented iron vessel save for a few stray drops that clung stubbornly to the scratched interior walls.

Stunned by the abrupt silence that followed the crash, Patty stood motionless beside the spreading puddle of spilled livelihood. She stared down at the wet soil where her ambitious financial empire had vanished into the porous ground without a trace. The vivid imagery of silk dresses and dancing fiddles dissolved like morning mist beneath the harsh glare of the climbing sun. A profound sense of grief washed over her, heavy and cold, replacing the buoyant optimism that had filled her chest only moments before. She realized with a bitter sting that her premature calculations had blinded her to the immediate responsibilities of the present moment.

Slowly, she retrieved the empty pail from the grass, its metallic clatter sounding remarkably hollow in the vast, indifferent countryside. The return journey was agonizingly long, each step back toward the cottage weighed down by the burden of her foolish imagination. She watched the sunlight glinting off distant roofs, feeling the sharp contrast between the bright exterior world and her own gloomy thoughts. Her mother would inevitably demand an explanation for the ruined morning and the empty vessel clutched in her trembling hands. How could she confess that a nonexistent flock of chickens and a nonexistent ballgown had caused the loss of their most urgent asset?

Upon crossing the threshold, her tear-stained face and silent posture communicated the disaster before she even uttered a single word. Her mother looked up from the wooden table, her eyes softening with immediate compassion as she surveyed the dented tin container. With a halting voice, Patty recounted the entire sequence of events, sparing herself none of the blame for her reckless daydreaming. She braced herself for a stern rebuke, expecting harsh words to match the severity of the financial loss inflicted upon the household. Instead, her mother merely sighed, extending a comforting hand to steady the trembling shoulders of the chastened girl.

Experience, the older woman remarked gently, is a stern master that exacts high tuition for the lessons it imparts. She reminded Patty that counting upon future fortunes before they are secured is a trap that has snared many an eager mind. The spilled milk could not be coaxed back into the pail, nor could yesterday's errors be undone by the simple shedding of tears. Absorbing these words like dry earth drinking rain, Patty felt the initial panic subside into a resolute, if sober, acceptance. She resolved to root her ambitions firmly in the tangible reality of the farm rather than the fleeting shadows of fancy.

Later that afternoon, she sat by the cottage window watching the swallows swoop low across the cultivated rows of the vegetable garden. The interior world of her mind was remarkably quiet now, stripped of the elaborate fantasies that had proved so disastrously fragile. She understood that sustainable success demanded continuous vigilance, patient labor, and an unwavering focus on the task directly at hand. Every movement from that day forward would be guided by common sense and a healthy respect for the unpredictability of fortune. The road to the market would test her patience again tomorrow, but she knew she would walk it with a steadier and wiser tread.

## st-0136 — The Bundle of Sticks
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
An old man lives in a house. He has young children. The children always play. They always move. They always make problems. They always come to a big place. They always do a bad thing. The children always go. They do bad things. The father looks at them. The father feels very bad. The father feels very bad every day. The father feels very bad all day. He wants a good change now.

One morning, he calls the children. He brings many things. The things are from wood. The things are in one big group. The group is large. He asks them to break it. Each child tries to break the group. They use all their power. The big group does not break. The big group stays in one.

The father opens the big group. He gives one thing to each child. They try again now. Every child breaks his thing well. Every child breaks it soon. The thing breaks into parts. It breaks with no problem.

The father explains the test to them. He talks about the group and parts. Together they are strong. They are strong when they stay together. They are not strong. They are not together. The children look at the father. The children understand the test well. They stop now. They live together well.

### A2
Once, an old man lived in a small house near the country. He had several sons. These young men always grew very bad with one another. Every single day, they would fight about little things. The father felt very bad because his family lacked support.

He wanted to teach them something good. One morning, he called all the brothers into his room. He brought a large bundle of sticks. The sticks were close together with strong lines. The father told them that people could break the group.

Each son tried very hard to break the sticks. The oldest brother pulled the bundle with all his force. It did not move. Then another brother took his turn and pushed hard against the material. None of them could break it.

After everyone failed, the old man opened the line. He separated the parts. He untied the rope and gave them single sticks. He asked them to try again. This time, every person broke his piece very easily.

The father looked at his sons. He explained the meaning of the test. He told them that if they stayed together, they would be strong. If they lived alone and fought, they would be low. The brothers finally understood that society gives them true force.

The family life became good. They did not fight at all. The brothers worked together every single day. The father felt very happy for his children. He knew they learned something very important.

### B1
Once upon a time, an old farmer lived in a small house near the country. He had several sons who always grew angry with one another. Every single day, these young men would argue about little things. The father felt very sad because his family lacked health and quiet. He wanted to teach them a lesson which they would never forget. One morning, he called all the brothers into his room. He brought a large collection of sticks which was securely joined with strong line. The father told them that any person who could break the collection. He promised that person would get a good gift. Each son tried very hard to break the joined sticks. The oldest brother pulled the collection with all his strength, but it did not move. Then another brother took his turn and pushed hard against the wood. However, no matter how much effort they made, none of them could break it.

After everyone failed, the old man separated the pieces. He gave a single stick to each son and asked them to try again. This time, every single person broke his stick very easily. The pieces separated apart within a second. The father looked at his sons and explained the meaning of the test. He told them that if they stayed together, they would be strong like the collection. If they lived alone and fought, they would be weak like the single sticks. The brothers finally understood that quiet gives them true strength. Now, the family has become very quiet. The brothers have learned how to share their lives without fights. If the father asks them to work together, they always do so. They have discovered that life is much better when everyone helps each other.

The old man is extremely happy because his sons have finally changed. Every day, they show great care and support for one another. They know that future problems will be easy to solve if they remain close. This important lesson has stayed in their minds for many years. The brothers often talk about that special morning test. They always remember what the single sticks and the collection taught them. Their community also notices the wonderful change in the family. People frequently support the brothers for their strong bond. The farmer feels proud when he sees his children living in complete quiet. Nothing can separate them now because they share a deep love.

They have realized that staying close is the right choice. Every single member of the family values this peace above everything else. The story of the sticks has traveled far across the country. Many other families have learned from this wonderful example. Parents often tell the story to their own children. They want their kids to understand the power of working together. Bond remains a central part of their daily life. The brothers continue to grow old while maintaining their strong bond. They face every challenge as a team rather than as separate individuals. Their story teaches an essential truth to all who hear it.

A family that stands together will always win difficult times. The old farmer rests quietly knowing his work is complete. The family will always stay strong if they keep working as a team. If the brothers share their love, they will never feel weak again. Every person in the house knows that their future depends on this bond.

### B2
Once upon a time, an old farmer lived in a small house near the country. He had several sons who always grew angry with one another. Every single day, these young men would argue about little things. The father felt very sad because his family lacked peace. He wanted to teach them a lesson which they would never forget. One morning, he called all the brothers into his room. He brought a large collection of sticks which was tightly tied with strong metal. The father told them that any person who could break the collection would receive a prize.

Each son tried very hard to break the sticks. The oldest brother pulled the collection with all his strength, but it did not bend. Then another brother took his turn and pushed hard against the wood. However, no matter how much effort they made, none of them could break it. After everyone failed, the old man freed the metal and separated the pieces. He gave a single stick to each son and asked them to try again. This time, every single person broke his stick very easily. The pieces snapped apart within a second.

The father looked at his sons and explained the meaning of the test. He told them that if they stayed together, they would be strong like the collection. If they lived alone and fought, they would be weak like the single sticks. The brothers finally understood that cooperation gives them true strength. They learned that working together makes their family safe and powerful. The old man was very glad to see this change in his children. He knew that life would bring many problems to them in the future.

If the brothers stayed divided, they would lose every single battle they faced. Therefore, they promised to support one another through all difficult times. The father smiled because his important lesson finally reached their minds. From that day forward, the young men stopped fighting over small matters. They lived in peace and supported each other like strong parts of a house. This ancient story shows how much power people have when they stand as one group. If people fight among themselves, they become weak and easy to defeat.

If they stay united, they can overcome any challenge that appears before them. The brothers shared everything they had and built a wonderful life together. Their children also grew up listening to this wise story about family cooperation. Every generation remembered the lesson of the sticks and the old farmer. They understood that division brings ruin while cooperation brings success. The farm became successful because all family members worked hard as a team. They never forgot the valuable lesson which their father taught them long ago.

Peace remained inside their home for many years until the end of time. This simple test changed their behavior and brought much joy to the family. Nobody ever argued about small problems again in that quiet country house. The brothers knew that cooperation was the best way to live. They worked on the farm every morning and shared their food every evening. If any problem appeared, they talked about it together until it was solved. The father watched them from his chair and smiled every single day.

### C1
In the quiet countryside where the shadows of ancient oaks stretched across the gravel paths, an old farmer spent his final autumns within the walls of a modest, timber-framed house. He had lived a long life tilling the stubborn soil, yet the greatest burden he carried was not the weight of the plow, but the constant discord that echoed among his several sons. Every single day, these young men found reasons to quarrel over trivial matters, their voices rising sharply above the quiet domestic routines. The father felt a deep, persistent sorrow settle within his chest because his family lacked even the simplest measure of peace. He knew that life would soon demand more resilience than they currently possessed, and he desperately wanted to teach them a lesson they would never forget.

One crisp morning, as the pale sunlight filtered through the dust-motes in the hallway, he called all the brothers into his shadowy room. They gathered reluctantly, their expressions hardened by years of petty rivalry and unspoken grievances. Resting on the wooden table before him was a large collection of sticks, bound tightly together with a strong band of wrought metal. The old man leaned forward, his weathered hands trembling slightly against the grain of the desk. He told them firmly that any person among them who could break the bound collection would receive a prize of significant value.

Intrigued by the challenge, each son stepped forward in turn, eager to demonstrate his physical superiority over the others. The oldest brother gripped the thick bundle with all his strength, his muscles straining against the unyielding wood, but it did not bend even a fraction of an inch. Then another brother took his turn, throwing his entire weight and pushing hard against the center of the stubborn timber. However, no matter how much effort they expended or how desperately they applied their leverage, none of them could make the bundle yield. Frustration flickered across their flushed faces as they realized their combined youth and strength were entirely useless against the bound obstacle.

After everyone had failed and stepped back in exhausted silence, the old man slowly freed the metal clasp and separated the rigid pieces. The individual branches fell apart with a soft clatter upon the wooden surface. He handed a single stick to each son and asked them to try again with the same task. This time, every single person broke his fragile stick very easily, applying barely a fraction of the force they had used before. The pieces snapped apart within a second, splintering loudly in the quiet room.

The father looked closely at his sons, his eyes holding a profound mixture of disappointment and enduring affection as he prepared to explain the meaning of the test. He told them plainly that if they stayed together, they would be strong like the collection of bound sticks. If they lived alone and fought constantly, they would be weak and vulnerable like the single sticks scattered on the floor. The brothers finally began to understand the quiet wisdom behind the demonstration and how cooperation provides true strength. They learned in that heavy silence that working together as a single unit makes their family safe and powerful against the hardships of the world.

The old man was deeply glad to see this sudden shift in the attitude of his children. He knew from his own long years that life would bring many difficult problems to their doorsteps in the future. If the brothers stayed divided by their petty anger, they would surely lose every single battle they faced in the harsh world outside. Therefore, they made a solemn promise to support one another through all the trials and difficult times that lay ahead. The father smiled because his important lesson had finally reached their minds and softened their stubborn hearts.

From that day forward, the young men stopped fighting over small matters that had previously consumed their daily conversations. They lived in remarkable peace and supported each other reliably, standing like the strong, interlocking parts of a well-built house. This ancient story shows how much power ordinary people possess when they stand together as one unified group instead of fighting among themselves. If people choose to fight within their own walls, they become weak and easy to defeat when external pressures arrive. If they stay united through adversity, they can successfully overcome any challenge that appears before them on their path.

The brothers shared everything they harvested and built a wonderful, prosperous life together on the ancestral land. Their own children also grew up listening to this wise story about family cooperation and mutual respect. Every single generation remembered the clear lesson of the sticks and the old farmer who taught it. They understood deeply that division brings inevitable ruin while cooperation brings lasting success. The farm became remarkably successful because all family members worked hard together as a cohesive team, leaving no room for selfishness.

They never forgot the valuable lesson which their father had taught them long ago in that dim room. Peace remained firmly inside their home for many years, lasting until the end of their days. This simple, brilliant test changed their behavior permanently and brought immense joy to the entire family. Nobody ever argued about small problems again in that quiet country house where discord once reigned supreme. The brothers knew from daily experience that cooperation was simply the best and most sensible way to live.

They worked diligently on the farm every single morning and shared their simple food every quiet evening. If any unexpected problem appeared on the horizon, they talked about it calmly together until it was completely resolved. The father watched them from his favorite wooden chair and smiled every single day, content that his legacy was secure. His heart was full as he witnessed the harmony that replaced the bitter arguments of the past. The house stood as a testament to the enduring power of unity in a troubled world.

## st-0137 — The Crow and the Pitcher
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
One day is very long. A man sees a small thing. The thing is an old person. The person has a problem. The person wants water. The light is up. The day is long. The person looks for water. The person goes across the land. The person looks here. The person looks there. The person wants to find water. The person looks every day. The person walks far. The person is very tired.

The person finds a large thing. It is a big pot. The big pot has water. The water is at the low level. The person wants to get the water. The person tries to reach the water. The water is too low. The person cannot reach it. The person looks at the water. The water is down. The person wants to take a bit of water. The person cannot do it now. The pot is too deep for the person.

The person does not stop. The person has an idea. The person looks down. The person sees small things. The person sees small bits. The bits are on the land. The person takes a bit. The person puts the bit in. The person puts more bits in. The person works hard. The person takes many small stones. The person drops every stone into the pot. The stones go to the bottom of the pot.

The water starts to rise. The water goes up. The water reaches the top. The person is very good now. The person can now take the water. The person gets the water. The person is well now. The problem is done. The person is happy. The person drinks the water. The day ends well for the person.

### A2
One hot summer day, a little animal flew across the land. He wanted to find some water to drink. After a long search, he discovered a big container. There was a little water at the base. The animal felt very happy because he hoped to drink. He was very happy to see the water. He wanted to drink right away. He needed to find a way to get it. The day was very hot, and he was tired. He looked around the whole place for help. He hoped to find some help quickly.

A big problem appeared when he tried to reach it. The area of the container was too small. The water level was too low for his face. He tried to push his head inside. He could not touch the drink. He tried many times to reach the water. Nothing happened because the level was too low. He felt bad about this problem. He did not want to leave the area. The water was right there, but he could not reach it. He tried to think of a plan.

Many other animals would have left the place. This good animal did not stop. He stood still and began to think about the problem. He wanted to find a way to fix it. He knew that he must not give up. He looked around the ground for something useful. He wanted to see if anything was there. His eyes looked at the hard ground. He knew that time was moving fast. He had to act now.

Soon, his eyes saw some small rocks on the ground. An idea came to him. He picked up a rock in his face part. He carried it gently toward the container. He wanted to put the rock inside. He knew what he was going to do next. He took another rock from the ground. He carried it up to the top. He worked hard to move the rocks.

The animal dropped the small rocks into the top. As each piece fell down, the water started to rise. The good animal continued this work. The water reached the top of the container. He watched the water go up step by step. He felt that success was coming soon. The water level became higher and higher. He watched the change with big eyes. He knew his plan was going to work.

Finally, the animal was able to drink easily. จบ He finished his problem and drank the water. This simple story shows that good thinking can fix problems. Everyone can learn a good thing from this day. He went home with a happy heart. He was very glad about his success. He flew back to his home. The day ended with a happy animal.

### B1
One hot summer day, a very active bird flew across dry land to find water. After a long search, he discovered a tall container. The container had a little water at the bottom. The bird was extremely happy because he hoped to drink and feel better. He wanted to feel good after such a long flight under the hot sun. The day was very difficult for any small animal. He looked around the dry land to see what else he might find. There was no river or lake near the open field. He only had this one chance to get some water. The bird flew down toward the container with great care. He felt sure that he could find a way to reach the water inside. His bright eyes looked at the bottom of the tall container. He saw the water waiting for him. It looked cool and clean. He knew that he must try to drink it. The sun continued to send hot light upon the dry ground. He needed to act fast before the water disappeared. His little heart beat fast as he landed near the container. He always worked hard when life became difficult.

However, a big problem appeared when he tried to reach the water. The top of the container was too narrow. The water level was much too low for his mouth. He tried to push his head inside. He could not touch the cool drink. Many birds would have given up and left the place in grief. This smart bird, who had a very strong mind, refused to stop. Instead of walking away, he stood quiet. He began to think about how he could solve the difficulty. He knew that a smart mind can find a way through any problem. He looked at the narrow top again and again. He tried to think of something useful to do. The water was right there, but he could not reach it with his mouth. He did not want to leave without a drink. His family always told him to be strong when things got hard. He remembered those good words now. He stayed right where he was to look for a solution. The hot wind blew across the dry ground. He had experienced many hard days before this moment. If he fails today, he will not survive the afternoon.

Soon, his bright eyes saw some small rocks resting on the ground near the green land. An idea came to him which would change the situation completely. He picked up a rock one by one. He held each piece in his mouth. He carried it carefully toward the container. With great care, the bird dropped the small rocks into the open top of the glass. As each piece fell down into the bottom, the water started to rise higher and higher. The smart animal continued this work until the water reached the top. Finally, the bird was able to drink easily. He could satisfy his terrible need for water. This simple story shows everyone that care and smart thinking can solve a very hard problem. When people use their minds, even the most difficult situation can be managed. The bird felt very proud of his work. He had solved the big problem all by himself. The cool water made him feel strong again. He flew away into the blue sky with a happy heart. His bright day ended with a great success. If he has time, he will tell his friends about this journey. He has learned that a smart mind brings great rewards.

### B2
One hot summer day, a very dry bird flew across the dry land to find some water. After a long search, he discovered a tall vessel that had a little liquid at the bottom. The animal was extremely happy because he hoped to drink and feel better. However, a big problem appeared when he tried to reach the liquid. The neck of the container was too narrow, and the water level was much too low for his face. He tried to push his head inside, but he could not touch the cool drink. Many birds would have given up and left the place in silence. This smart bird, who had a very strong mind, refused to stop. Instead of walking away, he stood quiet and began to think about how he could solve the difficulty. Soon, his bright eyes saw some small stones on the ground near the grass. An idea came to him which would change the situation completely. He picked up a stone one by one in his mouth and carried it carefully toward the pitcher. With great care, the bird dropped the small rocks into the open top of the glass. As each piece fell down into the bottom, the liquid started to rise higher and higher. The smart animal continued this work until the water reached the top. Finally, the bird was able to drink easily and satisfy his terrible need. This simple story shows everyone that careful thought and smart planning can solve a very hard problem. When people use their minds, even the most difficult situation can be overcome. The sun shone brightly upon the dry land as the bird worked. He knew that life depended upon finding a way to drink. Without water, no creature could survive the heat of the summer day. Therefore, every single action mattered greatly. He picked up another rock from the ground and carried it forward. The process was slow, but he did not stop for anything. He dropped the stone into the vessel, and the liquid rose slightly higher. This method was tested over and over again until success seemed possible. Other animals might have walked away from such a difficult challenge. Yet this bird understood that always working hard brings results. The water level moved closer to the top with every single piece added. He watched the liquid rise with a happy heart. His bright eyes remained fixed on the opening of the tall container.

The stones filled the bottom space, forcing the water higher into reach. No matter how hard the task was, he kept going forward without fear. His strong mind guided every movement he made during the long afternoon. At last, the goal was achieved because he refused to stop early. The cool drink was finally available for him to enjoy fully. He drank until his need was completely gone and he felt strong again. This amazing event proved that careful planning and effort always lead to success. People everywhere can learn a valuable lesson from this little bird. When faced with hard problems, one should always use reason and stay calm. Many stories are told about animals that show human qualities. This particular tale remains one of the most famous examples of smart behavior. Children and adults both enjoy reading about the journey of the bird. It teaches a major principle about how to handle difficult moments in life. Whenever a person encounters a wall, they should think like the bird. Instead of feeling sad, they must look for creative ways to move past the barrier. Small steps taken consistently can lead to massive improvements over time. Just as the small stones raised the water, small efforts build a better future. Everyone has the power to solve complex issues if they remain calm and patient. The world is full of challenges that require deep thought and steady action. If someone gives up too soon, they will never know what might have happened. Therefore, people should always keep trying even when the situation looks impossible. The bird in the story represents the best qualities of the human spirit. His actions show that a strong mind can overcome any physical wall. We should remember this lesson whenever we face trouble in our daily lives. Success belongs to those who do not let fear stop them from trying. Let us all follow this great example and work toward our goals every day. By doing so, we will find answers to the hardest questions we meet. The bird felt good after he drank. He worked hard to raise the water. His clever plan worked well in the end. Many people tell this tale to children today. Every person can learn from the animal. Patience and time bring good results. No challenge is too big if we stay calm.

Life is full of moments where we must think deeply. If we work hard, we can change our future. The small stones made a big difference in the end. We should never give up when things get hard. The bright sun shone down upon the green grass. Animals and people share many similar traits in stories. This famous tale will live on for many years. Children read about the bird and learn important lessons. Teachers share this book with students in school. Everyone loves a story about a smart animal. When we face trouble, we must use our minds. The pitcher was tall, but the bird was clever. He did not stop until the water was high enough. His actions proved that reason is powerful. Let us remember to stay strong during hard times. We can overcome any wall if we try. The journey of the bird teaches us to be patient. Small efforts add up to great success over time. May we all find ways to solve our problems. The world is a place of learning and growth. Every single day brings new chances to improve. Let us look forward to a bright tomorrow. The story ends with a happy bird and a clear lesson. We are all able to achieve our goals if we keep going. Thank you for reading this wonderful tale about the bird.

### C1
Beneath the merciless glare of a midsummer sky, a solitary corvid traversed a landscape stripped of its vitality by the relentless heat. The terrain stretched out before him in dusty shades of ochre and bone, offering neither shade nor respite from the stifling atmosphere. His throat burned with a fierce, desiccated ache that turned every beat of his wings into an exertion of sheer will. For hours, he had scavenged the parched earth, driven solely by the primal imperative to find moisture or perish. It was precisely when despair threatened to anchor his wings that his sharp eyes caught a faint glint of ceramic rising from the dirt ahead.

Approaching swiftly, he discovered a tall, narrow-necked vessel standing half-buried near the withered remnants of a forgotten garden. With a surge of anticipation that fluttered wildly in his chest, he peered over the rim and perceived a tiny pool of liquid shimmering at the bottom. The sight momentarily cured his exhaustion, filling his mind with the vivid promise of cool, life-giving water. Yet, no sooner had the hope bloomed than it was crushed by a cruel physical reality. The neck of the container was frustratingly slender, and the water level sat far below the reach of his eager beak.

He thrust his head downward, straining every muscle in his neck, but his skull met the unyielding clay wall long before his tongue could graze the surface. Again and again, he angled his body, twisting and stretching in desperate attempts to bridge the impossible gap between desire and fulfillment. The cool liquid mocked him with its silent presence, just inches beyond his grasp yet functionally as distant as the clouds. An ordinary creature of the wild would surely have surrendered to the tyranny of circumstance, turning away to accept a bleak fate in the dust. Not so this bird, whose intellect burned brighter than the sun overhead and refused to yield to an obvious defeat.

Standing perfectly still upon the sun-baked soil, he fixed his intelligent gaze upon the obstacle and began to dissect the problem with methodical precision. Random exertion had failed, which meant that only careful strategy could alter the immutable laws of the container. As his sharp eyes scanned the immediate surroundings, they fell upon a scattering of small, heavy pebbles half-embedded in the dry earth near a tuft of grass. A sudden spark of insight illuminated his thoughts, bridging the gap between what was and what could be. He realized that if the water could not rise to meet him, he would have to force the water to ascend by displacing the empty space it occupied.

Moving with deliberate care, he hopped over to the nearest stone and grasped it firmly within his dark beak. The pebble was surprisingly heavy, taxing his strength as he carried it back across the dirt toward the mouth of the tall pitcher. With meticulous precision, he dropped the small rock into the narrow opening, listening intently as it fell through the darkness. A dull, echoing splash signaled its arrival at the bottom, though the water level appeared frustratingly unchanged by the tiny addition. Undeterred by the microscopic margin of progress, the bird immediately turned back toward the grass to secure another piece.

Slowly, relentlessly, the arduous labor continued beneath the scorching afternoon heat. He picked up a third stone, carried it forward with aching muscles, and released it into the waiting vessel. With every single rock that tumbled into the depths, the displaced liquid crept upward by a fraction of a millimeter. Other animals might have abandoned such tedious work long before, but this bird understood that cumulative effort is the truest weapon against insurmountable odds. His bright eyes remained locked on the opening, measuring the minuscule gains that collectively spelled the difference between life and death.

The sun traced its slow arc across the sky, casting lengthening shadows over the dry land while the solitary worker maintained his steady rhythm. No matter how weary his wings felt or how dry his throat became, he refused to allow impatience to disrupt his methodical task. The pile of stones upon the ground dwindled as the invisible column of water inside the pitcher inched ever closer to the rim. He watched the liquid rise with a triumphant flutter in his breast, knowing that his intellectual persistence was finally bearing fruit. Every single action mattered in this grand design, proving that reason could conquer physical barriers that brute force could never dismantle.

At last, after what felt like an eternity of patient labor, the shining surface of the water crested near the top of the narrow neck. He peered inside and saw the cool, sweet liquid waiting right beneath the opening, entirely within his reach. With a profound sense of accomplishment, he lowered his beak and drank deeply, soothing the burning agony in his throat until his thirst was fully quenched. His strength rushed back into his limbs, replacing the exhaustion of the long afternoon with the vibrant energy of survival. This remarkable event stood as undeniable proof that careful thought and unwavering perseverance can overcome even the most daunting adversities.

Humanity has passed down this tale through countless generations because it captures a fundamental truth about existence. When people encounter massive walls in their daily lives, they are often tempted to surrender to despair. Yet the clever bird teaches us that no problem is truly absolute if one approaches it with a calm mind and a willingness to break a challenge into manageable parts. Small steps taken consistently can lead to monumental achievements over time, just as tiny pebbles raised the water in the pitcher. The world is full of challenges that demand deep thought rather than reckless action, rewarding those who refuse to let fear dictate their limits.

Teachers share this narrative with young minds in schools everywhere, ensuring that the lesson of the clever corvid endures across time. Children and adults alike find inspiration in the sight of a small creature outsmarting a harsh environment through pure reason. Every single day brings new chances to improve our circumstances, provided we possess the patience to work steadily toward our goals. By keeping a clear head during difficult moments, we unlock the hidden potential to alter our futures for the better. Let us remember this timeless example whenever we face adversity, knowing that success belongs to those who think deeply and act with quiet determination.
