## st-0146 — The Tale of Peter Rabbit
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Mother Rabbit gives a word to four children. She tells them. Do not go into Mr. McGregor place. She leaves home to do shop. Three good little children go to find food. Three little ones go to find food now. They look for food all day long. They look for food day after day. They want some food for every time. Mother Rabbit goes out of the house. She wants to buy food at the shop. She walks down the land to the shop. She leaves the little children at home. The four little children stay at home together. Three little children are good and kind. They do not go into Mr. McGregor place.

Bad Peter is very bad. He goes under the door to the place. He eats a lot of food right now. He eats a lot of food every minute. Mr. McGregor sees him right at this time. Mr. McGregor runs after Peter very fast. Peter is very bad and very small. He loses his little thing and food. He does not want to stop at all. He tries to move away from Mr. McGregor. Mr. McGregor runs down the land fast. Peter runs away from the big man. Peter is very small and very low. He runs across the land day by day.

At last Peter moves away and is free. He runs back home to the family. He is bad and out of power now. Mother puts him to bed in the room. She gives him a little food and water. His good family eat a lot of food. They eat food every night. They eat food and water together. Peter stays in bed for a long time. He does not go out of the room. He stays in bed until the morning comes. Mother takes care of him all night long. Peter gets a fact from this time. He does not go back to the place.

### A2
Mother has four little children. She talks to them. She gives them a message. She tells them not to go into the garden. The garden is for a man named Mr. McGregor. Mother must leave now. She is going to do some shopping. She walks away from home. The three good little children stay behind. They get food in the green field. They pick some food together. They are good young children. They work hard all morning. They find nice food for the family. They look for food everywhere. They want to help their mother. They pick radishes and lettuce. They are very busy all morning long.

Little Peter is not good today. Peter is a bad boy. Peter goes under the open door. He goes into the big garden. He eats a lot of food there. He eats much food until he is full. Mr. McGregor sees him near the ground. Mr. McGregor runs after Peter very fast. Peter feels very bad in his heart. He feels a lot of fear. He runs away very fast from the man. He loses his blue jacket. He loses his shoes too. He runs through the dark ground and floor. Mr. McGregor tries to catch the little rabbit. Peter is very scared. He runs as fast as he can. He does not look back at all.

At last, Peter gets free from the man. He races back home to his mother. He looks very low on energy. He feels bad about his action. His mother sees him arrive. His mother puts him to bed right away. She gives him food in bed. She gives him tea to drink. His good sisters enjoy the food together. They eat food with their mother in the room. Peter stays in bed all day long. He rests his head on the bed. He learns a big thing today. He will never go back to that place again. His mother is happy that he is safe. Peter sleeps for a long time.

### B1
Mother family member warns her four children not to go into the big house area. She has left to do her shopping today. The three good little kids gather sweet fruit and food. They have always known that this place is dangerous. Mother wants every child to stay safe at home. She tells them to be good while she is away. The children promise to listen to her words. They do not want any trouble or danger. They know that the world outside can be bad. So they stay near the front door for a time. They look around the green land and the tall trees. Everything seems very quiet and peaceful now. The sun shines brightly upon the soft grass. A small bird sings a lovely song up above. The children feel happy and calm during this morning. They have spent many hours playing games together. They have shared food and water with each other. Mother has told them stories about the big world. She has explained why certain places are not safe. Now she must walk down the long road to town. She has many things to buy for the family. She will return before the evening sun goes down. The children watch her leave with a smile. They wave their hands as she walks far away. Then the three good little ones find some food. They pick sweet fruit from the small bushes. They put the fruit into a big bag. They are happy because they have done well. They have followed every rule that mother made. They have not gone past the front open space. They have stayed away from the dark woods. They have kept their promise to the family leader. This makes mother very proud when she comes back. She will see that her kids have been good. She will give them a nice treat for dinner. They will have a wonderful evening together.

Bad Peter does not listen to the family warning. He goes under the open wire and runs fast. He runs into the food area of the man. He eats a lot of food and green leaves. He has never seen so much good food before. He eats until he feels very full indeed. Suddenly the angry man spots the young kid. The man gives a loud shout and runs fast. Peter is very scared as the man comes closer. He loses his little blue clothes and shoes. He runs as fast as he can from the man. He struggles to escape through a small hole. At last he moves free and races back home. He is worn out and very upset about everything. He is put to bed with a warm drink. His obedient sisters enjoy a fine meal. They eat food and milk with their sweet fruit. They are happy that they stayed good all day. Peter lies in his bed and rests his head. He thinks about the trouble he has caused. He knows that he made a big mistake today. He should have listened to his mother's words. He should have stayed with his good sisters. If he had stayed home, he would be happy. If he had not run away, he would be safe. Now he feels sick and sad in his room. His mother comes upstairs to check on him. She gives him a warm drink to help him feel better. She looks at him with a serious face. She tells him that he must never go back there. Peter nods his head and promises to change. He will never be bad like that again. He has learned a very hard lesson today. He closes his eyes and tries to sleep. The house becomes very quiet as night falls. The moon shines down upon the small village. Every child is finally resting in a warm bed. Mother sits by the fire and thinks about the day. She hopes that Peter will remember this lesson. She knows that children need time to grow up. She loves her family with all her heart. Tomorrow will bring a new and better day.

### B2
Mother Rabbit gives a serious warning to her four children about the danger of going into the garden of Mr. McGregor. She leaves her home to do her shopping. The three good little kids gather sweet food from the trees and plants in the open ground. They are happy while they work together. Peter, who is a bad boy, moves toward the gate. He goes under the gate and finds a lot of food in the soil. He eats a large amount of green plants and roots. Mr. McGregor spots him immediately. The man gives chase to the young animal. Peter is very scared and loses his small blue jacket and his shoes during his escape. He runs as fast as he can across the land. He tries to find a way out of the area. He moves quickly through the space between the plants. At last, he manages to get free from the fence. He races back home, feeling completely worn out and frightened by the situation. His mother puts him right to bed because he is sick. She gives him a cup of hot drink with a plant quality to help him feel better. Meanwhile, his good sisters enjoy a nice meal of bread, milk, and sweet food. They are happy because they listen to their mother and stay safe at home. The family lives near a small village in the country. Every morning, the mother makes sure that the children understand the rules. She tells them that the world outside can be dangerous for young animals. Mr. McGregor is known to be a hard man who does not like small animals in his space. He has a large area where he grows many different plants. People from the town often talk about the great danger of going near that place. Peter usually listens to stories about the farm, but today he makes a bad choice.

He thinks that nothing bad will happen if he stays for only a short minute. However, the situation changes very quickly when the owner appears. Peter has to run for his life across the open ground. His shoes fall off his feet while he moves. He leaves his little blue jacket behind on a sharp branch. The experience teaches him a hard lesson about following instructions. When he finally arrives at his house, he is tired and cold. His mother looks at him with concern and takes care of him through the night. She hopes that he will never make the same mistake again. The other children finish their meal and prepare for sleep. They know that following rules is important for their safety and happiness. Life in the small home returns to normal after the big scare. Peter rests in his bed and thinks about his bad adventure in the garden. Days pass, and the memory of the event starts to fade away. The young animals grow up and learn more about the world around them. They play games in the green grass and enjoy the warm sun. Mother Rabbit is proud of her family and keeps a close eye on them. She wants to ensure that they stay away from trouble in the future. Mr. McGregor sometimes looks around his land, but he sees no sign of the little person. The plants grow tall and strong in the rich soil. Spring turns into summer, bringing new life to the country. The children become wiser and more careful during their daily walks. They always remember the terrible day when Peter lost his clothes. That experience remains a central topic of conversation among the sisters.

Whenever they feel like breaking a rule, they think about the hot drink and the bed. It is a powerful reminder of what happens to bad children. Thus, quiet rules the little home in the big forest once again. Everyone lives happily, knowing that safety is better than any adventure. The story of Peter and the garden becomes a classic family tale. It is told again and again as the young ones grow older. They understand that listening to parents is the right path to follow. And so, the little family continues its quiet life far from danger. Peter learns his lesson and never goes back to the place where he should not go. If Peter had stayed home with his sisters, he would not have lost his blue jacket. If Mr. McGregor had not seen him, Peter might have eaten more green plants. The mother rabbit told her children that they must never visit the dangerous farm. The garden which belonged to Mr. McGregor was full of delicious vegetables. Peter was chased by the angry farmer who wanted to catch him. The small rabbit managed to escape because he ran very fast. When Peter returned home, his mother knew that he had disobeyed her. She took care of him all night while he rested in his comfortable bed. The other rabbits ate their bread and milk while Peter was sick. They were happy that they had listened to their mother's wise advice. Every young animal in the forest learned a valuable lesson from this event. They understood why rules were created by older and wiser family members. Life in the country continued with peace and happiness for many long years.

### C1
Beneath the roots of a very big fir tree, there lived a comfortable little family of rabbits whose daily routines were governed by caution. Every single morning before stepping out into the dewy grass, the mother rabbit made sure her four children understood the strict rules of the woodland. She told them that the world outside could be profoundly dangerous for young animals who lacked experience. Mr. McGregor, whose vast enclosure sat just beyond the lane, was known to be a remarkably hard man who utterly despised small creatures. People from the nearby town often whispered about the great perils of approaching that formidable boundary.

On this particular morning, Mother Rabbit gathered her basket and gave her children a final, very serious warning. She announced that they were free to go down the lane or wander into the open fields, but they must under no circumstances venture near Mr. McGregor's garden. Having imparted this vital instruction, she left her home and set off down the road to do her shopping. The three obedient little rabbits, Flopsy, Mopsy, and Cottontail, immediately understood the wisdom of her words. They gathered sweet food from the wild plants in the open ground, working together with quiet happiness.

Peter, however, was a rebellious boy who rarely considered the consequences of his own stubborn desires. While his sisters busied themselves safely, Peter moved deliberately toward the forbidden wooden gate. He squeezed his small body under the planks and suddenly found himself standing amidst an overwhelming abundance of rich soil and fragrant crops. Without a moment of hesitation, he began eating a large amount of green plants and crisp roots. He persuaded himself that nothing bad could possibly happen if he stayed for only a short minute.

The illusion of safety shattered instantly when a tall figure appeared between the rows of cabbages. Mr. McGregor spotted him immediately, leaping up from behind a cucumber frame and shouting in anger. The furious man gave chase to the young animal across the damp soil, brandishing a heavy rake. Peter was terribly scared and lost both his small blue jacket and his leather shoes during his panicked escape. He ran as fast as he possibly could across the cultivated land, his heart pounding against his ribs.

Desperate to find a way out of the area, Peter moved quickly through the dense labyrinths of green vegetation. He became hopelessly entangled in a gooseberry net, which caught the brass buttons of his precious coat. Friendly sparrows pleaded with him to exert himself further as the heavy footsteps of the farmer drew dangerously close. With one final, desperate burst of energy, he managed to wrench himself free from the tearing branches. He squeezed back beneath the garden gate, racing back home while feeling completely worn out and terrified by the ordeal.

When he finally crossed his threshold, he collapsed on the sandy floor of the burrow in a state of exhaustion. His mother looked at him with profound concern, immediately perceiving that he had disobeyed her explicit instructions. She put him right to bed because he was thoroughly sick from overeating and shivering with nervous shock. To soothe his upset stomach and calm his racing pulse, she carefully prepared a cup of warm chamomile tea. She administered the plant-based remedy with quiet firmness, hoping that this harsh lesson would finally sink in.

Meanwhile, his good sisters enjoyed a peaceful and orderly meal of bread, milk, and sweet blackberries. They felt deeply content because they had listened to their mother's advice and stayed safely inside their familiar world. Life in the small home gradually returned to normal after the enormous scare that had disrupted their afternoon. Peter rested in his cozy bed, staring at the earthen ceiling and thinking about his disastrous adventure in the vegetable patch. Days slowly passed, and the sharp memory of the event began to fade into the background of their daily lives.

The young animals grew up steadily, learning more about the complex world that stretched out beyond their burrow. They played gentle games in the tall green grass and basked comfortably in the warm afternoon sun. Mother Rabbit remained remarkably proud of her family, though she continued to keep a very watchful eye on them. She wanted to ensure that none of her children would ever wander toward trouble in the future. Mr. McGregor occasionally walked the perimeter of his land, but he never saw another trace of the mischievous trespasser.

Spring eventually turned into summer, bringing vibrant new life and rich scents to the surrounding countryside. The children became much wiser and far more cautious during their daily walks through the meadows. They always remembered the terrible day when Peter lost his clothes to the angry farmer. That frightening experience remained a central and recurring topic of conversation among the three sisters. Whenever they felt the slightest temptation to break a rule, they would instantly recall the bitter tea and the lonely bed.

It served as a powerful and permanent reminder of what happens to disobedient children who ignore parental wisdom. Thus, quiet rule reestablished itself over the little home beneath the fir tree once and for all. Everyone lived happily, knowing implicitly that safety and obedience were far better than any reckless adventure. The story of Peter and the forbidden garden quickly evolved into a classic family tale. It was told again and again as the young ones grew older, preserving the moral lesson for generations.

They fully understood that listening to older and wiser family members was the only correct path to follow. And so, the little family continued its quiet life far from the dangers of the human world. Peter learned his lifelong lesson thoroughly and never once went back to the place where he did not belong. If Peter had simply stayed home with his sisters, he would never have lost his favorite blue jacket. Life in the country continued with deep peace and enduring happiness for many long years to come.

## st-0147 — Around the World in Eighty Days
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Phileas Fogg is in London. He sits in a room. He makes a big game. He will go around the world. He says it takes eighty days. He believes his plan is good. He wants to win. He sits at a table. He talks to men. He says he can do it. The men look at him. They give money to him. Fogg is very sure. He has much money. He is ready to start.

Fogg has a new helper. His name is Passepartout. They leave by train. They go on a big road. They cross many different countries. They face bad rain. They have little time. Fogg always keeps moving forward. Passepartout runs with him. They take another train. They look at the land. They see many things. They move day and night. They do not stop. They must be fast.

A man follows Fogg. The man is from the government. The man thinks Fogg is bad. The man thinks Fogg is a bank robber. The man wants to stop Fogg. The man causes many problems. Fogg still goes across the world. The man watches every step. Fogg does not see him. Fogg just keeps going. The man writes a report. He waits for a sign. He wants to catch Fogg. Fogg is too fast.

Fogg meets a kind woman. Her name is Aouda. She needs help. She joins the group. They go across water and land. Fogg keeps everyone good. Aouda is very nice. She talks to Passepartout. They are good friends. Fogg helps her. She is happy now. They move together. They take another train. They see the sea. They go on a big boat.

Fogg comes back to London. He is just in time. He wins the big game. The long day is over. The men look at him. Fogg is very calm. He gets his money. Aouda is with him. Passepartout is also there. They are very happy. The story is end. Fogg is a good man. He does a great thing. Everyone looks at them.

### A2
Inside a big club in London, Phileas Fogg sat by himself. He made a plan to travel around the world in eighty days. Fogg believed that his plan would work well. He wanted to win a big prize. Many people thought he could not do it. He had a lot of money for the trip. He wanted to prove his point to everyone.

With his new friend Passepartout, he left at once. They went by train. They also went by other ways. They crossed many countries during the long trip. They faced bad air and long waits. Fogg kept moving forward without any pause. He did not want to lose any time on the way.

A police officer wrongly believed that Fogg was a bad man. The officer thought Fogg was a bank worker. This man followed Fogg closely from city to city. The officer tried to stop the trip at every moment. He wanted to catch Fogg before the end of the journey. Fogg did not know about this person.

Fogg also helped a kind traveler named Aouda during the trip. She joined them on the way. Together they continued across land and water. Fogg took good care of the group. He made sure that everyone stayed free from any issue. They walked through many dangerous places together.

Finally, Fogg arrived back in London. He came back just in time. He did not know about a time change. This little problem gave him one more day. He won his big prize at the very last second. Everyone was very happy for him in the end.

### B1
Inside a quiet club, the calm Phileas Fogg sat by himself. He made a great bet that he could travel around the world in eighty days. Fogg believed that his plan would succeed. If he works fast, he will win the game. He has made many plans to travel fast. Fogg has known that the world is very big. He has always wanted to prove his great idea. The club members watched him with much interest. They thought about the long journey ahead. Fogg has prepared for the trip with care. He has packed his money and clothes. His friends hoped that he would return safely. Fogg has always been a man of few words. He has never shown any fear about the future. The bet was very important to him. He has decided to leave London at once.

With his new man Passepartout, he left at once by train. They crossed many countries while they faced storms and bad delays. Fogg has kept moving forward without any stop. Passepartout has helped Fogg during every problem on the road. They have traveled through many strange places. The train moved fast across the land. They have seen many different people and towns. Fogg has paid for all tickets without any trouble. They have crossed rivers and hills on their way. Passepartout has watched the time every single hour. They have faced bad weather and long delays. Still, Fogg has kept his calm face. They have run from city to city with great speed. Fogg has never missed a single train. They have moved day and night to win the bet.

A police officer wrongly believed that Fogg was a bank robber. This man followed them closely from city to city. The officer tried to delay their journey at every moment. Despite trouble, Fogg pressed on. The officer has watched every move that Fogg made. He has thought that Fogg took money from a bank. The police have wanted to stop Fogg for a long time. The officer has tried to catch them at each station. Fogg has not known about this danger. He has kept his eyes on the road ahead. The officer has followed them across the land. They have moved from one town to another very quickly. The police man has waited for the right paper to stop them. Fogg has continued his trip despite these bad events. He has not let anything slow him down.

Fogg also helped a kind traveler named Aouda. She joined them, and together they continued across the water. Fogg has kept the group safe during problems. Aouda has thanked Fogg for his great help. They have traveled together across the blue water on a large ship. Aouda has become a good friend to both men. They have shared food and stories on the way. Fogg has protected her from all danger. They have crossed the great water without fear. Aouda has enjoyed the long journey with them. The group has grown closer during the trip. They have faced high waves and wind. Fogg has always stayed strong for the group. Aouda has helped them understand new places. They have moved forward day after day.

Fogg arrived back in London just in time. He won his big bet and proved his journey was possible. The club members have welcomed him back with great joy. Fogg has shown that his plan was right. He has won the money and the game. Aouda and Passepartout have stayed by his side. The police officer has found out the truth at last. Fogg has proved that he was never a bad man. They have lived a happy life after the long trip. Fogg has remembered every moment of the adventure. The whole world has talked about his great journey. He has shown that anything can happen with a good plan. Fogg has finally rested in his quiet home. He has won the greatest bet of his life. The story of his travel has become famous everywhere.

### B2
Inside a quiet London club, the calm and precise Phileas Fogg sat by himself. He made a great wager with other men that he could travel around the whole world in just eighty days. Although the task was very difficult, Fogg believed that his plan would succeed. If the weather remained good, he would reach every distant place. Every member in the room looked at him with serious interest. The men who were present at the club doubted his ability to finish such a long trip. However, Fogg was sure that he could win the bet. He made a promise which he intended to keep no matter what happened. Each person around him wondered if such a journey was truly possible. The club members talked quietly about the amazing proposal which Fogg had just declared. Many individuals believed that the plan was completely impossible. Yet, Fogg remained entirely confident in his own ability. He knew that every single detail of the route had to be considered carefully. If he failed, he would lose a lot of money. Therefore, he prepared his mind for the challenge ahead. The other men watched him with careful attention. They could not imagine how anyone might survive such a massive test of endurance. Fogg simply drank his tea and waited for the clock to strike the correct hour.

With his lively new French servant Passepartout, he set off at once by train and ship. They crossed many different countries while they faced storms, missed connections, and strange delays along the way. Because every minute mattered, Fogg kept moving forward without any pause. The train which they took moved fast across the land. When the ship sailed across the sea, the wind blew hard against the sides. Passepartout tried to help his master whenever a problem appeared. Although things went wrong, Fogg stayed calm and did not show fear. They traveled through mountains and across wide rivers. The servant worked hard to ensure that all bags were secure. Every city brought new experiences and unexpected challenges. Sometimes the connections were difficult to catch. If they missed a single connection, the entire schedule would be ruined. However, Fogg always found a way to solve each issue. He looked at his watch constantly to check the time. Passepartout admired his master because Fogg never panicked during a crisis. They moved from one continent to another with incredible speed. The journey tested their physical strength and mental focus. Despite the trouble, they kept going toward their destination.

However, a police officer who wrongly believed that Fogg was a running bank criminal followed him closely from city to city. This officer caused further trouble and tried to delay their journey at every possible moment. Despite the police, Fogg pressed steadily on across the world. The officer watched every move which Fogg made during the trip. If the officer had caught Fogg, the journey would have ended immediately. Fortunately, Fogg always managed to stay one step ahead of his pursuer. The detective suspected that the English gentleman had taken money from a bank. He sent messages to police stations around the globe in order to stop them. Every time Fogg arrived somewhere new, the officer was close behind. This constant pursuit added danger to an already difficult trip. If Fogg had known about the officer, he might have felt worried. Instead, he focused entirely on the schedule and the wager. Passepartout worried constantly that the police might arrest his master. Yet, the clever servant managed to avoid the officer at every turn. The chase continued across oceans and borders without rest.

Racing against the clock, Fogg also helped a kind traveler named Aouda who needed support during her travels. She joined them, and together they continued across oceans and land toward their final place. Fogg's care and skill ensured that the group stayed safe despite various problems. Aouda was very grateful for the help which she received from the men. She became an important part of the group as they traveled forward. If she had not met them, her life would have been much harder. The young woman had faced great danger before they rescued her. Fogg treated her with wonderful kindness and respect. She joined the two travelers as they crossed dangerous lands. Aouda added a warm presence to their small team. They shared stories and helped each other overcome various obstacles. If the group had not stayed together, they might have failed. Instead, their friendship grew stronger with each passing day. Aouda watched in awe as Fogg solved every travel problem. She knew that she was safe with such a capable leader.

Finally, thanks to a lucky mistake about the date, Fogg arrived back in London just in time. He won his big bet at the very last second and proved that his amazing journey was truly possible. The people who had doubted him were very surprised by his success. If he had arrived one minute later, he would have lost all his money. The club members applauded when Fogg walked through the door. It was a day which everyone would remember for a long time. They realized that the impossible task had actually been completed. The men at the club shook his hand and offered warm congratulations. Fogg smiled slightly and accepted the victory with calm grace. Passepartout and Aouda shared in the happy moment. It was the greatest adventure of their lives. If anyone else had tried such a trip, they surely would have failed. Only Fogg could have succeeded through sheer will and precise planning. The whole city talked about the incredible journey for months afterward. Fogg proved that any goal can be reached if a person remains calm and focused.

### C1
Inside the wood-paneled quiet of the Reform Club, Phileas Fogg sat in absolute stillness by the flickering hearth. With a precision that bordered on the mechanical, he placed his teacup down and made a staggering wager with the assembled men. He claimed that he could circumnavigate the entire globe in a mere eighty days. Although the task seemed overwhelmingly difficult, Fogg believed firmly that his meticulously calculated plan would succeed. Every member in the room looked at him with a mixture of disbelief and serious interest, doubting his ability to finish such a long trip.

Fogg, however, remained entirely sure that he could win the bet, possessing a quiet promise which he intended to keep no matter what happened. Each person around him wondered if such a journey was truly possible, murmuring quietly about the amazing proposal. Many individuals believed that the plan was completely impossible, yet Fogg remained entirely confident in his own ability. He knew that every single detail of the route had to be considered carefully, lest a minor oversight ruin everything. If he failed, he would lose a massive fortune, so he prepared his mind for the formidable challenge ahead.

The other men watched him with careful attention, unable to imagine how anyone might survive such a massive test of endurance. Fogg simply drank his tea and waited for the clock to strike the correct hour, showing no outward sign of hesitation. With his lively new French servant, Passepartout, he set off at once by train and ship into the foggy London evening. They crossed many different countries while they faced sudden storms, missed connections, and strange delays along the way. Because every single minute mattered, Fogg kept moving forward without any pause, treating time as his most precious currency.

The train which they took moved fast across the land, its iron wheels clicking a relentless rhythm against the tracks. When the ship sailed across the vast sea, the wind blew hard against the wooden sides, testing the vessel and its passengers. Passepartout tried to help his master whenever a problem appeared, eager to prove his worth on this grand adventure. Although things frequently went wrong, Fogg stayed remarkably calm and did not show a single drop of fear. They traveled through towering mountains and across wide rivers, watching the shifting landscapes blur into a continuous ribbon of motion.

The diligent servant worked hard to ensure that all their bags were secure, guarding their belongings with fierce dedication. Every city brought new experiences and unexpected challenges, testing their resourcefulness in unfamiliar streets. Sometimes the connections were terribly difficult to catch, requiring a breathless sprint through bustling stations. Had they missed even a single connection, the entire schedule would have been ruined beyond repair. However, Fogg always found a clever way to solve each issue, consulting his watch constantly to check the passing time.

Passepartout deeply admired his master because Fogg never panicked during a crisis, maintaining a glacial composure under pressure. They moved from one continent to another with incredible speed, crossing borders that had once seemed impassable. The exhausting journey tested both their physical strength and their mental focus to the absolute limit. Despite the mounting trouble, they kept going steadily toward their distant destination, driven by willpower alone. Unbeknownst to them, a persistent police officer who wrongly believed Fogg was a dangerous bank criminal followed them closely from city to city.

This officer caused further trouble and tried to delay their journey at every possible moment, hoping to secure an arrest warrant. Despite the hidden police presence, Fogg pressed steadily on across the world, entirely unaware of the shadow trailing him. The detective watched every single move which Fogg made during the trip, waiting for a single misstep. Had the officer managed to catch Fogg earlier, the journey would have ended in immediate disaster. Fortunately, Fogg always managed to stay one step ahead of his pursuer, slipping away before the trap could snap shut.

The detective suspected that the English gentleman had stolen money from a bank, sending urgent messages to police stations around the globe. Every time Fogg arrived somewhere new, the officer was close behind, adding a layer of hidden danger to an already difficult trip. If Fogg had known about the officer, he might have felt worried, but instead he focused entirely on the strict schedule. Passepartout worried constantly that the police might arrest his master, hiding his anxious glances from the unflappable gentleman. Yet, the clever servant managed to avoid the officer at every turn, keeping their perilous escape route intact.

Racing against the relentless clock, Fogg also helped a kind traveler named Aouda who desperately needed support during her travels. She joined them, and together they continued across oceans and land toward their final destination. Fogg's unwavering care and practical skill ensured that the group stayed safe despite various unexpected problems. Aouda was immensely grateful for the help which she received from the men, finding solace in their steady company. She quickly became an important part of the group as they traveled forward, bringing warmth to the chilly railway carriages.

Had she not crossed paths with them, her life would have been much harder, given the perils she had previously faced. The young woman had endured great danger before they rescued her, yet her spirit remained remarkably resilient. Fogg treated her with wonderful kindness and respect, never letting the urgency of the wager diminish his refined manners. They shared stories and helped each other overcome various obstacles, forging a deep bond in the crucible of travel. If the group had not stayed together through the hardships, they might have failed, but their collective strength carried them through.

Aouda watched in absolute awe as Fogg solved every travel problem with calm authority, knowing she was safe with such a capable leader. Finally, thanks to a lucky mistake about the crossing of the date line, Fogg arrived back in London just in time. He won his massive bet at the very last second, proving that his amazing journey was truly possible. The people who had doubted him were utterly surprised by his sudden success, staring at him in stunned silence. Had he arrived even one minute later, he would have lost all his money and his social standing.

The club members applauded loudly when Fogg walked through the door, creating a scene which everyone would remember for a long time. They realized that the seemingly impossible task had actually been completed through sheer determination and flawless planning. The men at the club shook his hand and offered warm congratulations, acknowledging his triumph with genuine respect. Fogg smiled slightly and accepted the victory with calm grace, treating the worldwide adventure as if it were a mere afternoon stroll. Passepartout and Aouda shared in the happy moment, knowing this was the greatest adventure of their lives.

## st-0148 — The Tortoise and the Hare
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
One day, a big creature live in a country. The creature is strong. He runs very good every time. He love to talk about the run. He looks at a small creature. The small creature is not strong. The big creature think the small creature is bad. The small creature feel the problem. He wants to make a change now.

One morning, the small creature look at the big creature. He asks the big creature for a play. The big creature think the game is little. All other people and friends come around. They want to see the big event today.

The play begin right now. The big creature run very good. He is far away from the start. He looks back at the small creature. The small creature is far behind him. The big creature decide to stop. He closes his eyes.

The big creature stay down on the land. He closes his eyes for a long time. The small creature never stop moving forward. He goes step by step every minute. He do not stop to stay at all. He feels no bad mind in himself. The small creature move past the big creature.

The big creature open his eyes up. He looks around the place. He sees his big problem now. He runs to the end point. It is too late to win. The small creature come to the line first.

Every person be glad for the winner. The big creature learn something new. Being big is not everything. Small and quiet people win the play.

### A2
Once upon a time, a fast animal lived in a green country. The animal loved to talk about his great power. He often laughed at another animal. He told everyone that this one was not good. The poor animal felt bad about the big problem. He decided to make a change. One fine morning, the small animal looked at the other one. He challenged him to a race. The fast animal laughed because the challenge was fun. All the other animals gathered to watch the big event. They wanted to see what would happen next. They came from every part of the country. They stood by the road to see the start. The sun was high in the sky. The air was nice and warm. Everyone was ready for the big day. The animals talked about who would win. Some people thought the fast one would take first place. Others were not sure what the future would bring.

At the sign, the fast animal ran far ahead. He ran so fast that he was miles away. The fast animal looked back and saw the other far behind. He was sure that he could not lose. The fast animal decided to take a nice sleep by the road. He lay down on the ground under a tree. He closed his eyes and went to sleep. He thought he had all the time in the world. He did not care about the race anymore. The sun moved across the sky. Hours passed while he slept in the shade. He dreamed about winning the game. He did not hear anything around him. He was deep in his dream. The road was empty for a long time. No one was near him. The wind blew softly through the leaves. It was a very peaceful place.

While the proud animal slept, the other one never stopped. He moved forward step by step. His speed was not high, but he kept going without rest. He walked past the sleeping one without making a sound. When the fast animal opened his eyes, he realized his big problem. He got up and ran toward the finish line. However, it was already too late to win. The fast animal could only watch the other cross first. He felt very bad about what happened. He stood by the finish line and looked at the winner. The winner was tired but happy. He did not give up when things were hard. He just kept walking until he reached the end. The crowd was very happy for the winner. They made a lot of noise to show support. The fast animal learned a good thing that day. He understood that being fast is not everything. Being able to keep going is more important.

### B1
Once upon a time, a very fast animal lived in a green country. This animal loved to talk about his great speed every single day. He often laughed at a slow animal named the other one. He always told everyone that the other animal was too slow. The poor person felt tired of the constant words. He decided to make a change in his life right now. One fine morning, the calm person looked at the proud runner. He challenged him to a run across the green land. The fast runner thought the challenge was very funny. He laughed because he felt certain that he would win. All the other animals gathered around to watch the big event. They wanted to see what would happen during the morning. At the signal, the fast runner went far ahead. He ran so fast that he was soon miles away. The runner looked back and saw that the other animal was far behind. He felt certain that he could not lose this test. The fast runner decided to take a rest by the road. He has spent much time running fast in his past days. If he runs again tomorrow, he will win the race.

While the proud runner slept, the slow animal never stopped moving. He walked on and on with quiet power. His speed was slow, but he kept going without any rest. Eventually, the quiet animal passed the sleeping runner without a sound. When the runner woke up, he looked around and realized his mistake. He jumped up and ran as fast as he could. However, it was already too late for him to win the race. The runner could only watch as the other animal crossed the finish line first. He has tried many times to run fast in his life. If he runs again tomorrow, he will win the race. Many people have watched this big event in the country. If they run together, they will see who is fast. The animal has trained hard for many months now. If he practices every day, he will achieve great things.

Everyone was happy for the winner of the great race. The fast runner learned a valuable lesson that day. He understood that being fast is not everything if you stop. The story proves that slow and careful work always wins. The animals have learned a good lesson from this race. If they work hard every day, they will achieve great things. The community has grown much stronger since that morning. If the animals stay together, they will feel very safe. The leader has spoken to all members of the group. If everyone listens carefully, the message will be clear. The old story has taught us an important point. If we remember this lesson, our lives will improve.

### B2
Once upon a time, a very fast animal lived in a green country. The animal loved to speak about his great power. He often laughed at a slow animal. He always told everyone that the other animal was too slow to go anywhere. The poor animal felt tired of the constant worry and decided to make a change. One fine morning, the calm animal looked at the proud one and challenged him to a race. The animal laughed loudly because he thought that the challenge was quite funny. All the other animals in the community gathered around to watch the big event. They wanted to see what would happen when the two animals started the game. Every person in the community talked about the upcoming race with great interest. If the fast animal had not been so proud, he might have acted differently. At the signal, the fast animal moved far ahead like a flash of light. He ran so fast that he was soon miles away from the starting line. The fast animal looked back and saw that the slow animal was far behind him. Certain that he could not possibly lose, the fast animal decided to take a lazy rest by the road. While the proud animal slept soundly, the steady animal never stopped moving forward. He moved on and on with a quiet determination, step by step. Though his pace was slow, he kept going without any rest or fear. Eventually, the steady animal quietly passed the sleeping competitor. He walked along the path while the sun shone high in the sky. Many hours passed by as the community waited for the result of the big event. People talked among themselves about who might win the race. Some individuals thought that the fast animal would finish first without any trouble. Others believed that anything could happen during such a long journey. The community members watched the distance with great interest and hope. If the fast animal had stayed awake, he would have won easily. Because he slept, he allowed his rival to pass him without knowing it. The steady animal showed that perseverance is more important than mere speed. Each step he took brought him closer to his ultimate goal. The people who watched from afar were amazed by his dedication. They whispered among themselves about the unexpected turn of events.

When the fast animal finally woke up, he looked around and realized his big mistake. He jumped up and ran as fast as he could toward the finish line. However, it was already too late for him to win the race. The fast animal could only watch as the animal crossed the finish line first. Everyone celebrated for the winner, and the animal learned a valuable lesson that day. He understood that being fast is not everything if you are too lazy to finish the work. The story proves that slow and steady always wins the race in the end. Life offers many chances to learn about calm work and hard effort. Every person should remember this important lesson when facing difficult problems. Success often comes to those who keep moving forward without stopping. The community celebrated the victory with great joy and happiness. Everyone agreed that the outcome was fair and right. The memory of that day remained in the hearts of the people for a long time. If anyone asks about the race, the villagers always tell this famous story. It is a tale which is passed down from parents to their children. The moral of the story is something that should never be forgotten by anyone. Hard work and dedication are values which are praised by all members of the society. Thus, the slow animal became a hero who was respected throughout the entire country. His victory proved that patience and effort can overcome natural talent. And so, the community lived happily while remembering the great lesson of that special day.

### C1
Once upon a time, across a valley bathed in the emerald light of a lush country, there lived a remarkably swift hare. Gifted with an extraordinary lightness of foot, the animal loved nothing more than to boast of his natural power. He constantly ridiculed a tortoise who moved slowly through the tall grass, finding endless amusement in the contrast. Day after day, the hare made sure everyone knew that the tortoise was simply too sluggish to ever accomplish anything of note. Worn down by the persistent mockery, the tortoise eventually decided that enough was enough.

One fine morning, under a sky of pale gold, the calm tortoise looked directly at the proud hare and challenged him to a race. The hare burst into loud, mocking laughter, finding the very notion of a contest between them utterly absurd. News of the impending trial spread quickly through the woodland community, drawing curious onlookers from every corner. All the animals gathered near the edge of the old road, eager to witness what promised to be a most peculiar spectacle. Talk of the upcoming event filled the morning air, with every creature whispering about the sheer audacity of the tortoise.

Had the hare not been blinded by his immense pride, he might have approached the challenge with caution. However, his absolute arrogance convinced him that defeat was a physical impossibility for someone of his talent. The tortoise, meanwhile, understood full well that arduous effort would be required if he hoped to achieve the impossible. He prepared himself mentally for the long and grueling journey, focusing his mind entirely on the path ahead. The spectators lined the dusty edges of the road, watching intently as the two competitors assumed their positions at the starting line.

An electric tension hung in the air as every spectator held their breath, waiting for the official signal to be given. At the sharp sound of the start, the hare propelled himself forward like a sudden flash of brilliant light. He ran with such ferocious speed that he vanished from sight within moments, leaving the starting line far behind. Stopping after a short while, the hare looked back and saw that his opponent was nowhere near. Convinced that victory was already his, the proud animal decided to take a leisurely and self-satisfied rest by the side of the road.

While the proud hare drifted into a deep sleep beneath the shade of a large oak tree, the steady tortoise never stopped moving. He pressed onward with a quiet, unwavering determination, advancing step by measured step without pause. Although his pace was undeniably slow, he maintained his rhythm without the slightest trace of fear or hesitation. Eventually, the steady tortoise quietly passed the sleeping competitor, walking right by him while the sun climbed higher into the sky. Hours slipped away as the community waited patiently near the finish line for news of the outcome.

Murmurs drifted through the gathered crowd as individuals debated who might eventually emerge victorious. Some animals maintained that the hare would surely appear at any moment and claim an effortless victory. Others whispered that anything could happen during a journey of such demanding length, keeping their hopes alive. They watched the distant horizon with intense concentration, wondering if the tortoise was still making progress. Had the hare remained awake and vigilant, he would have secured his triumph without breaking a sweat.

Because he chose to sleep away the afternoon, he allowed his humble rival to slip past him completely unnoticed. The tortoise demonstrated through his actions that true perseverance counts for far more than mere raw speed. Each deliberate step brought him closer to the ultimate goal that had once seemed completely out of reach. The spectators watching from the distance were utterly amazed by his steadfast dedication and refusal to quit. They whispered among themselves in sheer disbelief at the dramatic turn of events unfolding before their eyes.

When the hare finally awoke from his long slumber, he stretched lazily and looked around the quiet road. Sudden panic gripped him as he realized how much time had passed, and he jumped up to run toward the finish line. It was already far too late, however, for him to salvage the race or catch up to his rival. He could only watch in stunned silence as the tortoise crossed the finish line to the sound of roaring cheers. The community erupted in joyful celebration for the winner, and the hare learned a humbling lesson that very day.

He finally understood that natural speed means nothing if one lacks the discipline to finish what was started. The story proved beyond any doubt that slow and steady persistence will always triumph over arrogance in the end. Life presents countless opportunities to appreciate the value of calm work and genuine, honest effort. Every member of the community resolved to remember this vital lesson whenever they faced difficult challenges in the future. They understood that true success invariably belongs to those who keep moving forward through every obstacle.

The villagers celebrated the historic victory with great feasts and widespread happiness late into the evening. Everyone agreed that the outcome was entirely fair, a just reward for unwavering commitment and hard work. The memory of that special afternoon remained etched in the hearts of the people for many years to come. Whenever visitors asked about the famous race, the villagers never failed to recount the inspiring tale in full detail. It became a cherished story passed down from parents to their children across generations.

The core moral of the story was treated as a sacred truth that should never be forgotten by young or old. Hard work and dedication were recognized as virtues that elevated the spirit of the entire society. In time, the humble tortoise became a beloved hero who commanded deep respect throughout the entire country. His remarkable victory proved conclusively that patience and steadfast effort can overcome even the most formidable natural gifts. And so, the community continued to live happily, always remembering the powerful lesson taught on that memorable day.

The big man is here. The large man is on the land. He is very still. He is good. The day is long. A little friend is here. The small friend runs on the hand. The big man is up. He looks at the little friend. He takes the small friend. He holds it now.

### C1
Beneath the relentless blaze of the midday sun, the undisputed monarch of the wilderness stretched his immense frame across the golden earth, surrendering to the heavy pull of exhaustion. Silence enveloped the immediate clearing as the majestic beast drifted into a profound slumber, his formidable paws twitching occasionally with the phantom echoes of the hunt. It was precisely during this fragile interval of vulnerability that a diminutive rodent, scurrying frantically through the underbrush in search of sustenance, inadvertently crossed the vast expanse of the sleeping giant. Light as a falling autumn leaf, the tiny creature trod upon the magnificent chest, wholly unaware of the sleeping power beneath. Scarcely had the mouse reached the halfway mark when the great lion stirred, jolted abruptly from his dreams by the sudden, tickling intrusion.

With a swiftness that belied his massive bulk, a heavy, calloused paw slammed down, pinning the terrified trespasser against the dusty ground with inescapable force. Golden eyes, blazing with righteous indignation and startled irritation, glared down from a terrifying height upon the trembling captive. The tiny mouse, paralyzed by a mixture of awe and sheer terror, looked up into the terrifying maw of the forest king and managed to utter a desperate plea for mercy. Do not crush me, the desperate creature squeaked, trembling under the crushing weight, and if you spare my life this once, I swear I shall repay your incredible generosity. Amused by the sheer audacity of such an insignificant being offering assistance to the lord of beasts, the lion let out a low rumble of laughter.

Pity momentarily overriding his initial annoyance, the great predator lifted his heavy paw and watched as the little animal vanished into the safety of the surrounding brush in a frantic blur. Days slipped effortlessly into weeks, and the ordinary rhythms of the wilderness resumed their ancient, predictable course beneath the vast expanse of the open sky. Then fate intervened with sudden cruelty when the majestic lion, prowling through an unfamiliar sector of the dense forest, stepped blindly into a hidden snare woven from thick, fibrous ropes. No sooner had his weight shifted than the ingenious mechanism triggered, hoisting the powerful animal high into the air and binding his limbs tightly against his torso. He thrashed violently against the unyielding hemp, his muscles straining to their absolute limits, but the more he fought, the deeper the cruel bonds bit into his fur.

Despair replaced fury as the trapped monarch realized his immense physical strength was entirely useless against the manmade contraption. Resorting to his only remaining asset, he unleashed a series of thunderous roars that reverberated across the valleys and shook the trembling leaves from the high canopy. Miles away, resting quietly near the riverbank, the tiny mouse recognized the distinct, agonized timbre of that familiar voice echoing through the heavy air. Without a moment of hesitation, the small creature abandoned its foraging and darted swiftly toward the source of the distress, driven by an instinctive sense of profound obligation. Arriving at the scene, the mouse took in the horrifying sight of the great beast suspended helplessly in the lethal snare.

There is no time for hesitation, the brave little animal chirped, immediately scaling the taut ropes until it reached the thickest knot securing the primary suspension cable. With jaws that moved with astonishing speed and relentless determination, the tiny savior began to gnaw through the stubborn plant fibers, shredding the thick material piece by painstaking piece. The lion watched in breathless disbelief as the sharp teeth sliced steadily through the barrier that had defeated his own unmatched power. Suddenly, with a sharp snap that echoed like a whipcrack, the final strand parted, sending the massive predator crashing down onto the soft earth below. Instantly freed from the prison of hemp, the great beast stood tall once more, shaking the dust from his golden coat and staring down at his miniature benefactor with newfound reverence.

No longer did the lion view the world through the arrogant lens of mere physical might, for he had learned that true strength often resided in the most unexpected places. Together, the two vastly different creatures began the slow journey back toward the heart of the forest as the long afternoon shadows lengthened across the emerald grass. Cool evening breezes whispered through the overhead branches, carrying the sweet scent of damp earth and blooming wildflowers as the sun dipped toward the western horizon. They walked side by side in companionable silence, secure in the knowledge that genuine friendship transcends every barrier of size, status, and physical power. Nothing troubled the profound peace of the woods that evening, as all creatures large and small rested under the watchful gaze of the rising stars.

Generations would eventually pass, and the unique tale of the lion and the mouse would transform into a cherished legend whispered among the inhabitants of the great green land. Old mice gathered their attentive offspring in the cozy hollows of ancient trees, passing down the timeless truth that kindness is never wasted no matter how small the giver. Children listened with wide eyes, absorbing the fundamental lesson that arrogance blinds the mighty while humility opens the door to unexpected salvation. Wisdom thus flowed continuously from one season to the next, cementing the remarkable story into the very fabric of nature's eternal cycle. Whenever disputes arose among the diverse populations of the forest, someone would inevitably recount the famous rescue to restore harmony and mutual respect.

As night finally settled its gentle blanket over the landscape, the tired pair curled up near the roots of their favorite tree to seek well-earned rest. Visions of peaceful meadows and endless horizons danced through their slumbering minds, unt troubled by the anxieties of the waking world. They had proven beyond any doubt that cooperation and mutual care constitute the strongest armor against the unpredictable hardships of life. Morning would soon arrive with fresh opportunities to explore the vibrant world, but for now, the deep silence of the woods offered absolute comfort. No external force could disturb their profound tranquility, for they shared a bond forged in the crucible of genuine adversity.

Let us hold this simple yet profound lesson close within our hearts as we navigate our own complex journeys through an often unpredictable world. May every reader find loyal companions who stand beside them when the inevitable traps of existence threaten to overwhelm their spirit. The story concludes here, but its enduring spirit will continue to inspire compassion and understanding for as long as tales are told. Always remember to treat every individual with gentle respect, regardless of their apparent station or physical capabilities. The world invariably becomes a much brighter place when we choose to care deeply for one another without reservation.

That is the final, unchanging truth of our shared adventure today, echoing softly through the quiet glades long after the last page is turned. May your days be filled with as much genuine happiness and unexpected grace as the lives of our two remarkable heroes. We bid you a temporary farewell until another wonderful book opens its covers to welcome you back into the realm of timeless imagination. Always keep the flame of empathy burning brightly within your soul, for it is the greatest treasure anyone can ever hope to find. Goodbye for now, and may your own path through life be blessed with faithful friends and enduring peace.
